02

Authentication and Session

CRITICAL ai patterns Date.now() feeding a sessionid — predictable secret open-sse/executors/deepseek-web.ts:950

A non-cryptographic random source (Date.now()) is being used to produce sessionid. Predictable across processes and observable within minutes to a determined attacker.

sessionCache.set(userToken, { sessionId: created, createdAt: Date.now() });
Fix: Use crypto.randomBytes() / crypto.randomInt() (Node) or crypto.getRandomValues() (browser). For OTPs: crypto.randomInt(100000, 1000000). For tokens: crypto.randomBytes(32).toString('base64url').

CRITICAL ai patterns Wildcard CORS on an app with authentication open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-gemini-sse.ts:289

Access-Control-Allow-Origin: * lets any web page in a browser call this API. On an app with logged-in users this is catastrophic — combined with cookie or Authorization-header handling it enables full session hijack from any origin.

"Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*",
Fix: Restrict origin to an explicit allowlist of trusted domains. Never combine `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` with credentials.
